      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "import traceback\n",
        "import logging\n",
        "\n",
        "# Suppress CrewAI's colored logs and tracebacks to prevent truncation\n",
        "os.environ[\"OTEL_SDK_DISABLED\"] = \"true\"\n",
        "logging.getLogger(\"crewai\").setLevel(logging.ERROR)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Retrieve key\n",
        "api_key = os.environ.get(\"GEMINI_API_KEY\")\n",
        "\n",
        "from crewai import Agent, Task, Crew, LLM\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # Use the tested working Gemini 3.8 Flash LLM\n",
        "    gemini_llm = LLM(\n",
        "        model=\"gemini/gemini-3.8-flash\",\n",
        "        temperature=0.3,\n",
        "        api_key=api_key\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Explicit Gemini Embeddings config\n",
        "    embedder_config = {\n",
        "        \"provider\": \"google-generativeai\",\n",
        "        \"config\": {\n",
        "            \"model\": \"models/text-embedding-004\",\n",
        "            \"api_key\": api_key\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    researcher = Agent(\n",
        "        role=\"Research Analyst\",\n",
        "        goal=\"Find and verify factual claims about {topic}\",\n",
        "        backstory=(\n",
        "            \"You gather facts carefully. You only state things you can support, \"\n",
        "            \"and you clearly mark anything you could not verify.\"\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        llm=gemini_llm,\n",
        "        verbose=False,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    research_task = Task(\n",
        "        description=\"Research {topic}. Find the key facts a newcomer would need.\",\n",
        "        expected_output=\"A bulleted list of 6-10 facts. Mark any uncertain ones as UNVERIFIED.\",\n",
        "        agent=researcher,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    crew = Crew(\n",
        "        agents=[researcher],\n",
        "        tasks=[research_task],\n",
        "        verbose=False,\n",
        "        memory=False,\n",
        "        planning=False,\n",
        "        embedder=embedder_config\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"Starting asynchronous kickoff...\")\n",
        "    # Using top-level await to avoid any synchronous event loop blocks in Colab\n",
        "    result = await crew.kickoff_async(inputs={\"topic\": \"mobile money adoption in Nigeria\"})\n",
        "    print(\"--- SUCCESSFUL RESULT ---\")\n",
        "    print(result)\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(\"--- DETAILED EXCEPTION CAUGHT ---\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Exception Type: {type(e)}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Exception Message: {str(e)}\")\n",
        "    print(\"\\n--- FULL TRACEBACK ---\")\n",
        "    traceback.print_exc()"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },

            "Starting asynchronous kickoff...\n",
            "--- SUCCESSFUL RESULT ---\n",
            "Here is a factual briefing on mobile money adoption in Nigeria:\n",
            "\n",
            "* **Bank-Led Regulatory Origin vs. Telco-Led Shift:** Historically, the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN) mandated a bank-led mobile money model to protect the established banking sector, in contrast to East Africa's telco-led model (e.g., Safaricom’s M-Pesa in Kenya). This delayed telecom-driven mobile money for years until the CBN released guidelines for Payment Service Banks (PSBs) in 2018, formally opening the market to mobile network operators.\n",
            "* **Entry of Major Telcos (2022):** Major telecommunication providers officially rolled out commercial PSB operations in mid-2022 after obtaining final CBN operating licenses, notably MTN Nigeria (operating as MoMo PSB) and Airtel Africa (operating as SmartCash PSB).\n",
            "* **The NIBSS Centralized Payment Backbone:** Unlike mobile money ecosystems that operate within proprietary, closed-loop systems, Nigerian mobile money operates on an open, highly interoperable framework governed by the Nigeria Inter-Bank Settlement System (NIBSS) Instant Payment (NIP) switch, allowing immediate, low-cost inter-bank and inter-wallet transfers.\n",
            "* **Proliferation of Agent Banking Networks:** Physical agent networks—popularized through the Shared Agent Network Expansion Facility (SANEF) established in 2018—serve as the primary on-ramp and off-ramp for mobile money. POS (Point of Sale) agents operated by fintech platforms like Moniepoint, OPay, and PalmPay have largely substituted traditional bank branches for cash-in and cash-out services nationwide.\n",
            "* **Fintech Wallet Dominance:** Standalone fintech wallets (particularly OPay and PalmPay) have achieved dominant transaction volumes for peer-to-peer (P2P) transfers and merchant payments, outpacing both traditional commercial bank apps and telecom-led PSBs in day-to-day retail transaction frequency.\n",
            "* **The 2023 Cash Redesign Catalyst:** In early 2023, the CBN attempted a rapid demonetization policy (the Naira redesign), resulting in a severe cash shortage across the country. This crisis forced tens of millions of consumers and informal micro-merchants who previously dealt strictly in physical cash to adopt digital transfers, QR codes, and agent-assisted transactions.\n",
            "* **Inclusion Metrics (EFInA 2023):** According to the Enhancing Financial Innovation & Access (EFInA) Access to Finance Survey 2023, total financial inclusion in Nigeria rose to 74% (with 64% formally served), driven substantially by agent banking and mobile digital payment infrastructure rather than physical bank branches.\n",
            "* **Persistent Rural-Urban and Infrastructure Disparity:** Adoption remains geographically uneven; rural areas struggle with reliable cellular broadband connectivity (2G/3G prevalence vs. 4G/5G), liquidity management for remote cash-in/cash-out agents, and recurring USSD session failure rates.\n",
            "* **Market-Wide Active User Numbers:** *[UNVERIFIED]* While various industry reports and operator filings estimate that Nigeria has over 40 million uniquely active mobile money wallet users as of 2024, an audited, de-duplicated central registry verifying unique active individual users across all fintechs, PSBs, and traditional banks is not publicly published by regulatory authorities.\n"
          ]
        }
      ]

          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

        "id": "a220a82d",
        "outputId": "2e069517-6407-45ce-e73c-1b071367c060"

      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "api_key = os.environ.get(\"GEMINI_API_KEY\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure the Brief Writer Agent\n",
        "writer = Agent(\n",
        "    role=\"Brief Writer\",\n",
        "    goal=\"Turn research notes into a clear, short brief\",\n",
        "    backstory=(\n",
        "        \"You write plainly for a busy reader. You write ONLY from the notes \"\n",
        "        \"you are given. You never add facts of your own.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    llm=gemini_llm,\n",
        "    verbose=False,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure the Writing Task (which depends on the research_task output as context)\n",
        "writing_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=\"Write a brief on {topic} using only the research notes.\",\n",
        "    expected_output=(\n",
        "        \"A 250-300 word brief with three sections: \"\n",
        "        \"What it is, Why it matters, What to watch.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    agent=writer,\n",
        "    context=[research_task],\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Instantiate the multi-agent Crew with the custom Google embedder\n",
        "crew = Crew(\n",
        "    agents=[researcher, writer],\n",
        "    tasks=[research_task, writing_task],\n",
        "    verbose=False,\n",
        "    memory=False,\n",
        "    planning=False,\n",
        "    embedder=embedder_config\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Starting multi-agent asynchronous kickoff...\")\n",
        "# Run using kickoff_async\n",
        "result = await crew.kickoff_async(inputs={\"topic\": \"mobile money adoption in Nigeria\"})\n",
        "print(\"--- SUCCESSFUL BRIEF ---\")\n",
        "print(result)"
      ],
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",
          "text": [
            "Starting multi-agent asynchronous kickoff...\n",
            "--- SUCCESSFUL BRIEF ---\n",
            "### What it is\n",
            "\n",
            "Mobile money in Nigeria is a digital financial ecosystem driven by agent networks, fintechs, and telecommunications operators. Rather than relying solely on smartphone apps, Nigeria's model is anchored by hundreds of thousands of human agents operating Point-of-Sale (POS) terminals as neighborhood \"human ATMs\" under the Shared Agent Network Expansion Facility (SANEF). The sector shifted in 2022 when the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN) approved Payment Service Banks (PSBs) run by major telcos, notably MTN’s MoMo PSB and Airtel’s SmartCash PSB. Alongside telco PSBs, digital-first operators like OPay and PalmPay provide high-uptime payment rails, while Unstructured Supplementary Service Data (USSD) infrastructure ensures basic feature phone users retain transaction access.\n",
            "\n",
            "### Why it matters\n",
            "\n",
            "Mobile money has become the primary catalyst for expanding formal financial inclusion, which grew from 56% in 2020 to roughly 64% in 2023, according to EFInA data. A major turning point occurred during the early 2023 CBN demonetization exercise and Naira redesign, where an acute physical currency shortage forced millions of informal merchants and unbanked citizens to adopt digital wallets. During this surge, fintech operators captured substantial market share from legacy commercial banks by delivering reliable transaction processing with far fewer system failures during peak demand.\n",
            "\n",
            "### What to watch\n",
            "\n",
            "Adoption remains uneven across demographics and geography. Digital financial inclusion is concentrated in southern and urban regions, while northern and rural areas—particularly rural women—experience disproportionate exclusion due to lower literacy, limited phone ownership, and cultural barriers. Preserving USSD access will be vital for serving these feature-phone populations. Furthermore, while the Nigeria Inter-Bank Settlement System reports mobile transfers reaching tens of trillions of Naira annually, the exact share of consumer retail spending settled via mobile wallets versus cash remains unverified due to the massive size of the informal economy.\n"
          ]
        }
      ]

        "writer = Agent(\n",
        "    role=\"Brief Writer\",\n",
        "    goal=\"Turn research notes into a clear, short brief\",\n",
        "    backstory=(\n",
        "        \"You write plainly for a busy reader. You write ONLY from the notes \"\n",
        "        \"you are given. You never add facts of your own.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    llm=llm,\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "writing_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=\"Write a brief on {topic} using only the research notes.\",\n",
        "    expected_output=(\n",
        "        \"A 250-300 word brief with three sections: \"\n",
        "        \"What it is, Why it matters, What to watch.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    agent=writer,\n",
        "    context=[research_task],\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Added the explicit Google embedder to avoid OpenAI fallback error\n",
        "crew = Crew(\n",
        "    agents=[researcher, writer],\n",
        "    tasks=[research_task, writing_task],\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        "    embedder=embedder_config\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Fixed the syntax error and changed to kickoff_async to avoid the Colab event loop crash\n",
        "result = await crew.kickoff_async(inputs={\"topic\": \"mobile money adoption in Nigeria\"})"

      "source": [
        "reviewer = Agent(\n",
        "    role=\"Fact Reviewer\",\n",
        "    goal=\"Check the brief against the research notes and reject anything unsupported\",\n",
        "    backstory=(\n",
        "        \"You are strict and specific. You do not approve work to be polite. \"\n",
        "        \"When a claim has no support in the notes, you say so and name the claim.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    llm=llm,\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "review_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=(\n",
        "        \"Check the brief against the research notes. For EACH check, state PASS or FAIL:\\n\"\n",
        "        \"1. Does every factual claim in the brief appear in the research notes?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"2. Is any number or date stated without support in the notes?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"3. Are all three required sections present?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"4. Does the brief assert anything the researcher marked UNVERIFIED?\\n\\n\"\n",
        "        \"If any check FAILS, list the exact problem claims and return a corrected brief. \"\n",
        "        \"If all pass, return the brief unchanged with APPROVED at the top.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    expected_output=\"The four checks with PASS/FAIL, then the final brief.\",\n",
        "    agent=reviewer,\n",
        "    context=[research_task, writing_task],\n",
        "    output_file=\"brief.md\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Added the explicit Google embedder to avoid OpenAI fallback error\n",
        "crew = Crew(\n",
        "    agents=[researcher, writer, reviewer],\n",
        "    tasks=[research_task, writing_task, review_task],\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        "    embedder=embedder_config\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Changed to kickoff_async to avoid the Colab event loop crash\n",
        "result = await crew.kickoff_async(inputs={\"topic\": \"mobile money adoption in Nigeria\"})\n",
        "print(result)"

              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #00ff00; text-decoration-color: #00ff00\">market share by offering fast transaction settlements, subsidized transfer fees, and low failure rates. The </span>   <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #00ff00; text-decoration-color: #00ff00\">infrastructure relies heavily on physical Point of Sale (POS) agents acting as \"human ATMs\" under the Shared </span>  <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",

            "### What it is\n",
            "\n",
            "Mobile money in Nigeria has expanded beyond legacy bank-led frameworks into an ecosystem driven by telecommunications firms and independent fintech operators. Between 2020 and 2022, the Central Bank of Nigeria (CBN) granted Payment Service Bank (PSB) licenses to telecommunications operators, including MTN (MoMo PSB) and Airtel (SmartCash PSB). In parallel, fintechs such as OPay and PalmPay gained substantial market share by offering fast transaction settlements, subsidized transfer fees, and low failure rates. The infrastructure relies heavily on physical Point of Sale (POS) agents acting as \"human ATMs\" under the Shared Agent Network Expansion Facility (SANEF), alongside USSD technology for feature-phone users and smartphone applications.\n",
            "\n",
            "### Why it matters\n",
            "\n",
            "Adoption accelerated sharply in early 2023 when the CBN’s Naira redesign and demonetization policy caused an acute physical cash shortage, compelling millions of merchants and consumers to embrace digital wallets. Nigeria Inter-Bank Settlement System (NIBSS) data reveals that mobile inter-scheme transaction volumes and values expanded multiple times over between 2021 and 2023, signaling a structural move away from cash. This shift drove measurable financial inclusion. According to EFInA’s 2023 survey, Nigeria's financial exclusion rate fell from 32% in 2020 to 26% in 2023, with formal financial inclusion rising to 64%, largely attributed to digital payment channels and agent networks.\n",
            "\n",
            "### What to watch\n",
            "\n",
            "* **KYC Regulatory Enforcement:** In late 2023, the CBN mandated Tier-1 Know Your Customer (KYC) rules requiring accounts to link a Bank Verification Number (BVN) or National Identity Number (NIN), resulting in freezes on non-compliant accounts in early 2024.\n",
            "* **USSD Channel Stability:** USSD remains critical for feature-phone access and areas with poor connectivity, but it faces pressure from recurring commercial disputes between commercial banks and telecom operators over network session fees.\n",
            "\n",

              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">Agent Network Expansion Facility (SANEF), alongside USSD technology for feature-phone users and smartphone </span>    <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",

              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">Adoption accelerated sharply in early 2023 when the CBN’s Naira redesign and demonetization policy caused an </span>  <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">acute physical cash shortage, compelling millions of merchants and consumers to embrace digital wallets. </span>      <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">Nigeria Inter-Bank Settlement System (NIBSS) data reveals that mobile inter-scheme transaction volumes and </span>    <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">values expanded multiple times over between 2021 and 2023, signaling a structural move away from cash. This </span>   <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">shift drove measurable financial inclusion. According to EFInA’s 2023 survey, Nigeria's financial exclusion </span>   <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">rate fell from 32% in 2020 to 26% in 2023, with formal financial inclusion rising to 64%, largely attributed </span>  <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>  <span style=\"color: #c0c0c0; text-decoration-color: #c0c0c0\">to digital payment channels and agent networks.</span>                                                                <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",
              "<span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>                                                                                                                 <span style=\"color: #008000; text-decoration-color: #008000\">│</span>\n",

      "source": [
        "### Resolving 429 Quota Exceeded with Rate Limiting\n",
        "\n",
        "To prevent hitting the 20 requests-per-minute limit of the Gemini free tier, we can set `rate_limit_per_minute` on our LLM instances. This configures CrewAI to automatically pace its calls."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 1000
        },
        "id": "3b44ee6e",
        "outputId": "1e68a60b-5157-4cab-921d-c6bc2b6c1e2b"
      },
      "source": [
        "import os\n",
        "from crewai import Agent, Task, Crew, LLM\n",
        "\n",
        "api_key = os.environ.get(\"GEMINI_API_KEY\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure LLM with a safe rate limit (e.g., 10 requests per minute)\n",
        "gemini_llm_throttled = LLM(\n",
        "    model=\"gemini/gemini-3.8-flash\",\n",
        "    temperature=0.3,\n",
        "    api_key=api_key,\n",
        "    rate_limit_per_minute=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure the Brief Writer Agent using the throttled LLM\n",
        "writer = Agent(\n",
        "    role=\"Brief Writer\",\n",
        "    goal=\"Turn research notes into a clear, short brief\",\n",
        "    backstory=(\n",
        "        \"You write plainly for a busy reader. You write ONLY from the notes \"\n",
        "        \"you are given. You never add facts of your own.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    llm=gemini_llm_throttled,\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure the Fact Reviewer using the throttled LLM\n",
        "reviewer = Agent(\n",
        "    role=\"Fact Reviewer\",\n",
        "    goal=\"Check the brief against the research notes and reject anything unsupported\",\n",
        "    backstory=(\n",
        "        \"You are strict and specific. You do not approve work to be polite. \"\n",
        "        \"When a claim has no support in the notes, you say so and name the claim.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    llm=gemini_llm_throttled,\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configure Tasks\n",
        "research_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=\"Research {topic}. Find the key facts a newcomer would need.\",\n",
        "    expected_output=\"A bulleted list of 6-10 facts. Mark any uncertain ones as UNVERIFIED.\",\n",
        "    agent=researcher,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "writing_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=\"Write a brief on {topic} using only the research notes.\",\n",
        "    expected_output=(\n",
        "        \"A 250-300 word brief with three sections: \"\n",
        "        \"What it is, Why it matters, What to watch.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    agent=writer,\n",
        "    context=[research_task],\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "review_task = Task(\n",
        "    description=(\n",
        "        \"Check the brief against the research notes. For EACH check, state PASS or FAIL:\\n\"\n",
        "        \"1. Does every factual claim in the brief appear in the research notes?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"2. Is any number or date stated without support in the notes?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"3. Are all three required sections present?\\n\"\n",
        "        \"4. Does the brief assert anything the researcher marked UNVERIFIED?\\n\\n\"\n",
        "        \"If any check FAILS, list the exact problem claims and return a corrected brief. \"\n",
        "        \"If all pass, return the brief unchanged with APPROVED at the top.\"\n",
        "    ),\n",
        "    expected_output=\"The four checks with PASS/FAIL, then the final brief.\",\n",
        "    agent=reviewer,\n",
        "    context=[research_task, writing_task],\n",
        "    output_file=\"brief.md\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build Crew\n",
        "crew_throttled = Crew(\n",
        "    agents=[researcher, writer, reviewer],\n",
        "    tasks=[research_task, writing_task, review_task],\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        "    embedder=embedder_config\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Starting rate-limited multi-agent kickoff...\")\n",
        "result = await crew_throttled.kickoff_async(inputs={\"topic\": \"mobile money adoption in Nigeria\"})\n",
        "print(\"--- RESULT ---\")\n",
        "print(result)\n"
      ],
      "execution_count": 42,
